- **screen-spec**: Generate screen specifications (IA / User Flow / Screen Spec / Wireframe / Dev Handoff) from an existing PRD. Trigger keywords: - Commands: "/screen-spec", "화면정의서 만들어줘", "화면 명세 만들어줘", "와이어프레임 만들어줘" - Natural language (바이브 코더 친화): - "화면 어떻게 생겼는지 보여줘", "UI 정의해줘" - "와이어프레임 그려줘", "프로토타입 만들어줘" - "화면별로 정리해줘", "페이지 명세 만들어줘" - "PRD 다음 단계", "화면 만들기 전에 정리" ([wigtn/wigtn-plugins](https://github.com/wigtn/wigtn-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/wigtn-plugins/commands/screen-spec.md) · ★45)
- **screen-spec**: Generate a screen specification from a Figma frame using MCP tools (get_design_context, get_screenshot). Produces a spec doc matching the SCREEN_SPEC.md template with layout sketch, components used, spacing observed, data dependencies, copy, accessibility notes, interactions, and error states. Use when documenting a specific screen. Do not use when the screen has no Figma frame or when the spec already exists. For 6 or more screens, use screen-spec-fleet. Once screens document raw values, use extract-foundations-from-screens to converge tokens. ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/screen-spec.md) · ★6)
- **screen-spec**: PRD/요구사항을 화면 설계서(스크린 스펙)로 구체화한다 ([sminju98/pm-copilot](https://github.com/sminju98/pm-copilot/blob/HEAD/commands/screen-spec.md) · ★3)
- **screen-spec-fleet**: Orchestrator-workers variant of screen-spec. Dispatches N Sonnet workers (1 screen each) in parallel to generate per-screen spec docs from Figma frames, then merges SCREEN_MAP.md and routes.md from per-worker partials. Use when screen count &gt;= 6 AND Figma MCP is available AND foundations + SCREEN_MAP exist. Do not use for single screens (use screen-spec), when foundations are missing (use design-bootstrap first), or when frames have not been enumerated yet (caller must supply screen_number+slug+persona+node_id per screen). ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/screen-spec-fleet.md) · ★6)

- **sdd-db-reverse**: Reverse engineering d'UN objet SQL exécutable — procédure stockée, fonction, vue ou trigger (P0.1 2026-07-24) — en lecture seule via la connection string de stack.md. Output workspace/plans/{n}-{FeatName}.analysis.md, consommé par /sdd-reverse-stories (3b). ([zekiriabd/SDD-Pro](https://github.com/zekiriabd/SDD-Pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdd-reverse-analyze.md) · ★193 · 2 copies)

Lit us/{n}-{m}-{Name}.md + plans/{n}-{FeatName}.analysis.md, écrit feats/{n}-{FeatName}.md consommable par /sdd-full. Séquence init + inventory + audit + paradigm + extraction (escalier 3a→3b→3c) + crosscut + review + synth + UI + questions + status — tous PAR DÉFAUT (décision Tech Lead 2026-06-13), opt-out via --skip-* / --minimal. SEULE exception : la parité (Phase 3.8) est opt-in via --with-parity (et ses artefacts vont dans workspace/parity/, jamais dans qa/).
